# Define the start and end dates for the 3-month period
start_date_val = DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of day'), '-3 months')
end_date_val = DATETIME('now', 'start of day')

# Find the top 3 sales representatives
top_sales_reps_by_revenue = Dealership.Salespersons.CALCULATE(
    # Select salesperson's first and last name
    sales_rep_first_name=first_name,
    sales_rep_last_name=last_name,
    # Calculate total revenue from sales made within the defined period
    # Revenue is defined as the sum of sale_price
    total_revenue_in_period=SUM(sales_made.WHERE(
        (sale_date >= start_date_val) & (sale_date <= end_date_val)
    ).sale_price),
    # Calculate the total number of sales made within the defined period
    total_sales_in_period=COUNT(sales_made.WHERE(
        (sale_date >= start_date_val) & (sale_date <= end_date_val)
    ))
).TOP_K(
    3,  # Select the top 3
    by=total_revenue_in_period.DESC()  # Order by total revenue in descending order
)

[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by claude:
# Filter payments from the previous ISO week and group by date and payment method
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    # Get payments from 14 days ago to 7 days ago (previous ISO week)
    (DATEDIFF("days", payment_date, DATETIME("now")) >= 7) &
    (DATEDIFF("days", payment_date, DATETIME("now")) < 14) &
    # Ensure we're getting the correct ISO week by checking day of week alignment
    (DAYOFWEEK(payment_date) >= DAYOFWEEK(DATETIME("now", "start of week", "-7 days"))) &
    (DAYOFWEEK(payment_date) <= DAYOFWEEK(DATETIME("now", "start of week", "-1 day")))
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date_only = payment_date,
    payment_method_type = payment_method,
    amount = payment_amount
)

# Group by date and payment method to get daily totals
result = previous_week_payments.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date_only, payment_method_type)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date = payment_date_only,
    payment_method = payment_method_type,
    daily_total = SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

# Calculate date boundaries for the previous ISO week.
# 'start of week' typically refers to Monday in ISO context.
# prev_week_start_date will be Monday of the previous week.
# prev_week_end_date will be Sunday of the previous week.
date_info = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    prev_week_start_date_calc=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    prev_week_end_date_calc=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 day')
)

# Filter payments received within the calculated previous week.
filtered_payments_received = date_info.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= prev_week_start_date_calc) & (payment_date <= prev_week_end_date_calc)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by payment_date and payment_method.
# Then, calculate the sum of payment_amount for each group.
# The fields payment_date and payment_method in the CALCULATE after PARTITION refer to the partition keys.
# PaymentsReceived.payment_amount refers to the original collection's field for aggregation within the partition group.
daily_payments_by_method = filtered_payments_received.PARTITION(
    name="payment_groups", by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    grouped_payment_date=payment_date,
    grouped_payment_method=payment_method,
    total_daily_amount_calc=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# Select and rename the final fields for the output.
result = daily_payments_by_method.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=grouped_payment_date,
    payment_method=grouped_payment_method,
    total_amount=total_daily_amount_calc
)

# Define the start and end dates of the previous ISO week.
# 'start of week' in DATETIME truncates to the beginning of the week (Monday by ISO standard).
# prev_week_start_date_calc will be the Monday of the previous week.
# prev_week_end_date_calc will be the Sunday of the previous week.
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    prev_week_start_date_calc=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    prev_week_end_date_calc=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 day')
)

# Filter payments received that fall within the calculated previous week.
payments_in_prev_week = date_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= prev_week_start_date_calc) & (payment_date <= prev_week_end_date_calc)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by payment_date and payment_method
# to group them for daily aggregation per payment method.
# Then, calculate the sum of payment_amount for each group.
result = payments_in_prev_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_group", by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    payment_type=payment_method,
    total_amount_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_day.ASC(), payment_type.ASC())

sales_2023_info = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter=((MONTH(sale_date) - 1) // 3) + 1,
    quarter_start_date=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year', 
                               JOIN_STRINGS(' ', ((((MONTH(sale_date) - 1) // 3) * 3), 'months')),
    sale_amount=sale_price
)

).ORDER_BY(customer_state.ASC(), quarter_start_date.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q61] DataFrame from claude is None
[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter sales for 2023 and prepare data for grouping.
# This includes extracting the customer's state and calculating the first day of the quarter for each sale.
sales_data_for_grouping = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    # Get the state of the customer who made the purchase.
    customer_state_attr=customer.state,
    # Calculate the first day of the quarter for the sale_date.
    # (MONTH(sale_date) - 1) // 3 determines the quarter (0 for Q1, 1 for Q2, 2 for Q3, 3 for Q4).
    # Multiplying by 3 gives the number of months to add to the start of the year (0, 3, 6, 9).
    quarter_start_date_attr=DATETIME(
        DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year'),  # Get the first day of the year of the sale.
        # Add the calculated number of months to get the first day of the quarter.
        '+ ' + STR(((MONTH(sale_date) - 1) // 3) * 3) + ' months'
    ),
    # Keep the sale price for later summation.
    sale_price_attr=sale_price
)

# Step 2: Group by customer state and the calculated quarter start date.
# Then, calculate the sum of sales for each group.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_data_for_grouping.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_sales_group",
    by=(customer_state_attr, quarter_start_date_attr)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer_state_attr,
    quarter_start_date=quarter_start_date_attr,
    # Sum the sale_price from the original Sales collection context within each partition group.
    total_quarterly_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

# Step 3: Order the results for better readability (e.g., by state and then by quarter).
quarterly_sales_report = quarterly_sales_by_state.ORDER_BY(customer_state.ASC(), quarter_start_date.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter sales for 2023 and calculate initial necessary fields.
sales_filtered = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    c_state=customer.state,
    s_price_val=sale_price,
    s_date_month=MONTH(sale_date),
    s_date_year_start=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year') # First day of the year of the sale
)

# Step 2: Calculate the quarter start date using the fields from the previous step.
sales_info = sales_filtered.CALCULATE(
    c_state=c_state, # Pass through customer state
    s_price_val=s_price_val, # Pass through sale price
    # Determine the first date of the quarter based on the sale month
    q_start_date=IFF(
        s_date_month <= 3, s_date_year_start, # Q1
        IFF(
            s_date_month <= 6, DATETIME(s_date_year_start, '+3 months'), # Q2
            IFF(
                s_date_month <= 9, DATETIME(s_date_year_start, '+6 months'), # Q3
                DATETIME(s_date_year_start, '+9 months') # Q4
            )
        )
    )
)

# Step 3: Partition by customer state and quarter start date, then calculate total sales for each group.
quarterly_sales_by_state_result = sales_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_group",  # Name for the partition context
    by=(c_state, q_start_date)   # Partition keys: customer state and quarter start date
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=c_state,      # Output field: customer's state (from partition key)
    quarter_start_date=q_start_date, # Output field: quarter start date (from partition key)
    total_sales=SUM(sales_info.s_price_val) # Output field: sum of sale prices for the group
)

# Assign the final result to the 'result' variable.
result = quarterly_sales_by_state_result

    sale_month_num = MONTH(sale_date),
    # Determine the first date of the quarter for each sale
    q_start_dt = IFF(sale_month_num <= 3, DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year'),
                  IFF(sale_month_num <= 6, DATETIME(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year'), '3 months'),
                    IFF(sale_month_num <= 9, DATETIME(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year'), '6 months'),
                                           DATETIME(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of year'), '9 months'))))
)

# Partition by customer state and quarter start date, then sum sales for each group
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_details_for_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_group", by=(cust_s, q_start_dt)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state_val = cust_s,
    quarter_first_date = q_start_dt,
    total_sales_amt = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

result = quarterly_sales_by_state

[DEBUG] [Q63] Code generated by gemini:
result = Drugs.CALCULATE(
    drug_name=drug_name,  # Select the drug name
    average_ddd=AVG(      # Calculate the average DDD for this drug
        # Access treatments where this drug was used
        treatments_used_in
        # Filter for treatments that have a valid end_date and where end_date is not before start_date
        .WHERE((HAS(end_dt)==1) & (end_dt >= start_dt))
        .CALCULATE(
            # Calculate the duration of the treatment in days.
            # "total days of treatment" is interpreted as inclusive of start and end days.
            # e.g., start_dt=Jan 1, end_dt=Jan 1 -> DATEDIFF is 0, so 0+1=1 day.
            # e.g., start_dt=Jan 1, end_dt=Jan 2 -> DATEDIFF is 1, so 1+1=2 days.
            # This also ensures duration is at least 1, preventing division by zero.
            treatment_duration_days_calc = DATEDIFF("days", start_dt, end_dt) + 1,

            # Calculate DDD for this specific treatment: total drug amount / total days of treatment
            ddd_value_calc = tot_drug_amt / treatment_duration_days_calc
        ).ddd_value_calc  # Select the calculated DDD value for this treatment to be averaged
    )
)

    drug_name = drug_name, # Select the drug name
    average_DDD_value = AVG( # Calculate the average DDD for the drug's treatments
        treatments_used_in.WHERE(
            HASNOT(end_dt) == 0 # Filter 1: Ensure end_dt is not null
        ).CALCULATE(
            # Calculate treatment duration in days and carry forward tot_drug_amt
            treatment_duration_days = DATEDIFF("days", start_dt, end_dt),
            treatment_tot_drug_amt = tot_drug_amt # Explicitly carry tot_drug_amt
        ).WHERE(
            treatment_duration_days > 0 # Filter 2: Ensure duration is positive
        ).CALCULATE(
            # Calculate DDD for this individual valid treatment
            individual_ddd = treatment_tot_drug_amt / treatment_duration_days
        ).individual_ddd # Provide the individual DDD values to the AVG function
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q64] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Define date boundaries using the top-level DermTreatment collection.
# These boundaries will define the overall 3-month window we are interested in.
date_info = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    current_month_start_dt_val = DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    three_months_ago_start_dt_val = DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of month'), '-3 months')
)

# Step 2: Prepare treatment data.
# We extract necessary fields and create helper fields for filtering, grouping, and aggregation.
treatments_prepared = date_info.Treatments.CALCULATE(
    p_key = patient_id,  # Alias for patient_id for clarity in aggregations
    treatment_ym_sort_key = YEAR(start_dt) * 100 + MONTH(start_dt),  # Numeric key for sorting months (e.g., 202305)
    treatment_ym_display_key = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),  # Display key for month (e.g., "2023-05")
    actual_start_dt = start_dt,  # Original start date for filtering
    # This field will be patient_id if the drug is biologic, otherwise NULL.
    # NDISTINCT on this field will count distinct patients with biologic treatments.
    biologic_patient_key_if_any = KEEP_IF(patient_id, drug.drug_type == 'biologic')
)

# Step 3: Filter treatments to include only those within the last 3 full months (excluding the current month).
treatments_in_period = treatments_prepared.WHERE(
    (actual_start_dt >= three_months_ago_start_dt_val) & (actual_start_dt < current_month_start_dt_val)
)

# Step 4: Partition the filtered treatments by month.
# The partition keys (treatment_ym_sort_key, treatment_ym_display_key) are from the treatments_in_period collection.
# Inside CALCULATE, 'Treatments' refers to the sub-collection of treatments_in_period for that specific month group.
monthly_aggregated_data = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_treatment_groups",  # Name for the partitioned groups
    by=(treatment_ym_sort_key, treatment_ym_display_key)  # Partition by both sort and display keys for month
).CALCULATE(
    month_sort_val = treatment_ym_sort_key,  # Pass through the sort key
    month_display_val = treatment_ym_display_key,  # Pass through the display key
    # Count distinct patients who had any treatment in this month
    total_distinct_patient_num = NDISTINCT(Treatments.p_key),
    # Count distinct patients who had at least one biologic treatment in this month
    biologic_distinct_patient_num = NDISTINCT(Treatments.biologic_patient_key_if_any)
)

# Step 5: Select the final fields for the output and order by month descending.
final_result = monthly_aggregated_data.CALCULATE(
    month = month_display_val,
    patient_count = total_distinct_patient_num,
    biologic_treatment_count = biologic_distinct_patient_num
).ORDER_BY(month_sort_val.DESC())

# Define date boundaries for the last 3 full months
current_month_start_dt = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
# The period of interest starts 3 months before the current month's start
# and ends just before the current month's start.
three_months_ago_start_dt = DATETIME(current_month_start_dt, '-3 months')

# Prepare treatment data: filter for the relevant period, extract month-year, and flag biologic treatments
treatments_with_info = DermTreatment.Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_start_date = start_dt, # Keep original start_dt for filtering
    # Create a sortable year-month string like "YYYY-MM"
    treatment_year_month_str = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_identifier = patient_id, # Use an alias for patient_id
    # Flag if the treatment used a biologic drug
    is_biologic_drug_treatment = (drug.drug_type == 'biologic')
).WHERE(
    # Filter treatments to be within the last three full months
    (treatment_start_date >= three_months_ago_start_dt) & (treatment_start_date < current_month_start_dt)
)

# Partition by month-year and calculate distinct patient counts

    name="treatments_by_month", by=(treatment_year_month_str) # Group treatments by the year-month string
).CALCULATE(
    month_period = treatment_year_month_str, # Output the month period
    # Count distinct patients who had any treatment in this month
    total_distinct_patients_in_month = NDISTINCT(treatments_by_month.patient_identifier),
    # Count distinct patients who had a biologic drug treatment in this month
    biologic_distinct_patients_in_month = NDISTINCT(
        KEEP_IF(treatments_by_month.patient_identifier, treatments_by_month.is_biologic_drug_treatment == True)
    )
).ORDER_BY(month_period.DESC()) # Order results by month, most recent first

[DEBUG] [Q71] Running models for: What are the PMPD and PMTC for each of the last 12 months, not including the current monthPMPD = per month patient diagnoses. PMTC = per month treatment count. Truncate start_dt to month for aggregation.
[DEBUG] [Q71] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q71] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q71] Code generated by claude:
# Get treatments from the last 12 months (excluding current month)
treatment_info = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 12 months, excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", treatment_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) >= 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", treatment_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) <= 12)
)

# Group by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
result = treatment_info.PARTITION(name="months", by=treatment_month).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),  # Per Month Patient Diagnoses
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)                  # Per Month Treatment Count
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q71] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q71] Code generated by gemini:
# Calculate the start of the current month and the start of the month 12 months prior.
# These define the boundaries for our 12-month analysis window, excluding the current month.
current_month_start_val = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
twelve_months_ago_start_val = DATETIME(current_month_start_val, '-12 months')

# Process the Treatments collection.
# 1. For each treatment, derive 'treatment_month_year_val' by truncating 'start_dt' to the start of its month.
# 2. Filter treatments to include only those within the last 12 full months (i.e., >= 12 months ago and < current month).
treatments_in_period = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month_year_val = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
).WHERE(
    (treatment_month_year_val >= twelve_months_ago_start_val) &
    (treatment_month_year_val < current_month_start_val)
)

# Partition the filtered treatments by 'treatment_month_year_val' to group them by month.
# Then, for each month, calculate PMPD and PMTC.
# PMPD: Number of distinct patients who received a diagnosis (via a treatment) in that month.
# PMTC: Total number of treatments administered in that month.
# The results are ordered by month in ascending order.
monthly_diagnoses_and_treatments = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_group", by=(treatment_month_year_val)
).CALCULATE(
    month_period = treatment_month_year_val,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month_period.ASC())

# Get the last 12 months excluding current month
treatment_info = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
).WHERE(
    # Filter for last 12 months excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", treatment_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) >= 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", treatment_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) <= 12)
)

# Group by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC

# Calculate the start of the current month and the start of the month 12 months prior.
# These define the boundaries for our 12-month analysis window, excluding the current month.
current_month_start_val = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
twelve_months_ago_start_val = DATETIME(current_month_start_val, '-12 months')

# Process the Treatments collection.
# 1. For each treatment, derive 'treatment_month_year_val' by truncating 'start_dt' to the start of its month.
# 2. Filter treatments to include only those within the last 12 full months (i.e., >= 12 months ago and < current month).
treatments_in_period = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month_year_val = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
).WHERE(
    (treatment_month_year_val >= twelve_months_ago_start_val) &
    (treatment_month_year_val < current_month_start_val)
)

# Partition the filtered treatments by 'treatment_month_year_val' to group them by month.
# Then, for each month, calculate PMPD and PMTC.
# PMPD: Number of distinct patients who received a diagnosis (via a treatment) in that month.
# PMTC: Total number of treatments administered in that month.
# The results are ordered by month in ascending order.
monthly_diagnoses_and_treatments = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_group", by=(treatment_month_year_val)
).CALCULATE(
    month_period = treatment_month_year_val,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month_period.ASC())

# Step 1: Define time window constants using the top-level collection.
# const_current_month_start will be the first day of the current month.
# const_twelve_months_ago will be the first day of the month, 12 months before the current month.
time_context = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    const_current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    const_twelve_months_ago = DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of month'), '-12 months')
)

# Step 2: Filter treatments based on the time window and prepare for aggregation.
# - Filter Treatments whose start_dt is within [const_twelve_months_ago, const_current_month_start).
# - Create a 'treatment_month' field by truncating start_dt to the first day of its month.
#   The original patient_id from Treatments is implicitly carried forward.
treatments_for_analysis = time_context.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= const_twelve_months_ago) & (start_dt < const_current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

# Step 3 & 4: Partition by the calculated 'treatment_month' and compute PMPD and PMTC.
# - 'month_col' will store the month for which metrics are calculated.
# - PMPD: Count of distinct patient_id values among treatments for that month.
# - PMTC: Total count of treatments for that month.
# The results are ordered by month in ascending order.
monthly_analysis_results = treatments_for_analysis.PARTITION(name="monthly_data", by=(treatment_month)).CALCULATE(
    month_col = treatment_month,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month_col.ASC())

# Step 1: Find each patient's first treatment year
patient_first_treatment = Patients.WHERE(
    HAS(treatments_received) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    patient_id=patient_id,
    first_treatment_year=YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group by year and count new patients per year

    name="years", by=first_treatment_year
).CALCULATE(
    year=first_treatment_year,

).ORDER_BY(year.ASC())

    year=year,
    number_of_new_patients=new_patients_count,

# Step 1: For each patient, find the date of their very first treatment.
# We partition the Treatments collection by patient_id and find the minimum start_dt for each patient.
patient_earliest_treatment_info = Treatments.PARTITION(name="patient_treatment_group", by=(patient_id)).CALCULATE(
    patient_identifier = patient_id,
    first_treatment_date = MIN(Treatments.start_dt) # Gets the minimum start_dt for treatments within this patient's group
)

# Step 2: Extract the year of this first treatment for each patient.
# This collection will effectively list each patient and the year they became a "new patient".
new_patient_registration_years = patient_earliest_treatment_info.CALCULATE(
    # patient_id_ref = patient_identifier, # This field is not strictly needed for the next step if COUNTing the collection
    year_became_new_patient = YEAR(first_treatment_date)
)

# Step 3: Group by the year of first treatment and count the number of new patients for that year.
# Order by year to ensure correct operation of the PREV function later.
yearly_new_patient_summary = new_patient_registration_years.PARTITION(name="year_group", by=(year_became_new_patient)).CALCULATE(
    year_value = year_became_new_patient,
    num_new_patients_for_year = COUNT(new_patient_registration_years) # Counts distinct patients who started in this year_group
).ORDER_BY(year_value.ASC())

# Step 4: Calculate NPINPI (New Patients Increase).
# NPINPI = current year's new patients - previous year's new patients.
# The PREV function looks up the value from the previous row after ordering by year.
npi_report_final = yearly_new_patient_summary.CALCULATE(
    year = year_value,
    number_of_new_patients = num_new_patients_for_year,
    NPINPI = num_new_patients_for_year - PREV(num_new_patients_for_year, by=year_value.ASC(), default=0)
)

# Step 1: For each patient with treatments, determine the year of their first treatment.
# This creates a collection where each row corresponds to a patient and includes the year of their first treatment.
patient_first_treatment_dates = Patients.WHERE(HAS(treatments_received) == 1).CALCULATE(
    # patient_id is implicitly part of each record from Patients.
    # We calculate first_treatment_year_calc for each patient based on the earliest treatment start date.
    first_treatment_year_calc = YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group patients by the year of their first treatment.
# Count the number of new patients for each year and order by year.
yearly_new_patient_counts = patient_first_treatment_dates.PARTITION(
    name="patients_by_first_treatment_year", by=(first_treatment_year_calc)
).CALCULATE(
    year_val = first_treatment_year_calc,  # The year of first treatment.
    # COUNT(Patients) counts the number of original Patient records that fall into this partition group
    # (i.e., patients whose first treatment was in year_val).
    num_new_patients_for_year = COUNT(Patients)
).ORDER_BY(year_val.ASC()) # Order by year is essential for the PREV function to work correctly.

# Step 3: Calculate the New Patients Increase (NPI) for each year.
# NPI is the difference between the current year's new patients and the previous year's new patients.
npi_results = yearly_new_patient_counts.CALCULATE(
    year = year_val,  # Final column name as requested: 'year'.
    number_of_new_patients = num_new_patients_for_year,  # Final column name as requested: 'number_of_new_patients'.
    # The PREV function looks up the value of num_new_patients_for_year from the previous row,
    # based on the ascending order of year_val.
    # 'default=0' handles the first year in the series, where there's no preceding year.
    NPI = num_new_patients_for_year - PREV(num_new_patients_for_year, by=year_val.ASC(), default=0)  # Final column name as requested: 'NPI'.
)

result = npi_results

# Step 1: Create a contextless expression for outcomes with non-null PASI scores
# for both day 7 and day 100. This collection will be used for calculating averages.
# We assume '!= None' is the standard way to check for non-null values in PyDough.
filtered_pasi_outcomes = Outcomes.WHERE(
    (day7_pasi_score != None) & (day100_pasi_score != None)
)

# Step 2: Calculate the overall D7D100PIR.
# We start from the top-level 'DermTreatment' collection to perform a global calculation.
d7d100pir_calculation = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the average PASI score on day 7 using only the filtered outcome records.
    avg_pasi_d7 = AVG(filtered_pasi_outcomes.day7_pasi_score),
    # Calculate the average PASI score on day 100 using only the filtered outcome records.
    avg_pasi_d100 = AVG(filtered_pasi_outcomes.day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    # Apply the D7D100PIR formula.
    # Use IFF to handle potential division by zero if avg_pasi_d7 is 0.
    # The result is multiplied by 100.0 to express it as a percentage.
    calculated_d7d100pir = IFF(
        avg_pasi_d7 != 0,
        (avg_pasi_d100 - avg_pasi_d7) / avg_pasi_d7 * 100.0,
        None  # Return None if avg_pasi_d7 is 0, as the rate is undefined.
    )
)

# Step 3: Select only the final percentage value as requested.
result = d7d100pir_calculation.CALCULATE(
    D7D100PIR_percentage = calculated_d7d100pir
)

    (DEFAULT_TO(day7_pasi_score, -1.0) != -1.0) & (DEFAULT_TO(day100_pasi_score, -1.0) != -1.0)
)

# Step 2 & 3: Calculate overall average PASI scores from the filtered outcomes at the DermTreatment graph level,
# then calculate the D7D100PIR using these averages.

    # Calculate average PASI scores for day 7 and day 100 from the valid outcomes.
    avg_day7_pasi_val = AVG(valid_outcomes_for_pasi.day7_pasi_score),
    avg_day100_pasi_val = AVG(valid_outcomes_for_pasi.day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    # Apply the D7D100PIR formula.
    # Handle cases where avg_day7_pasi_val is null or zero to avoid errors.

        (avg_day100_pasi_val - avg_day7_pasi_val) / avg_day7_pasi_val * 100.0,
        None # Return None if avg_day7_pasi_val is null or zero, indicating undefined PIR
    )
)

    d7d100pir = (
        AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.WHERE(
            (day7_pasi_score != None) & (day100_pasi_score != None)
        ).day100_pasi_score) - 
        AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.WHERE(
            (day7_pasi_score != None) & (day100_pasi_score != None)
        ).day7_pasi_score)
    ) / AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.WHERE(
        (day7_pasi_score != None) & (day100_pasi_score != None)
    ).day7_pasi_score) * 100
)
[DEBUG] [Q75] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q75] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Define a collection of outcomes that have non-null PASI scores for both day 7 and day 100.
# PASI scores are non-negative (0-72), so -1.0 is a safe marker for null.
valid_outcomes_for_pasi = Outcomes.WHERE(
    (DEFAULT_TO(day7_pasi_score, -1.0) != -1.0) & (DEFAULT_TO(day100_pasi_score, -1.0) != -1.0)
)

# Step 2 & 3: Calculate overall average PASI scores from the filtered outcomes at the DermTreatment graph level,
# then calculate the D7D100PIR using these averages.
result = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    # Calculate average PASI scores for day 7 and day 100 from the valid outcomes.
    avg_day7_pasi_val = AVG(valid_outcomes_for_pasi.day7_pasi_score),
    avg_day100_pasi_val = AVG(valid_outcomes_for_pasi.day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    # Apply the D7D100PIR formula.
    # Handle cases where avg_day7_pasi_val is null or zero to avoid errors.
    d7d100pir_percentage = IFF(
        (DEFAULT_TO(avg_day7_pasi_val, -1.0) != -1.0) & (avg_day7_pasi_val != 0.0), # Check avg_day7_pasi_val is not null and not zero
        (avg_day100_pasi_val - avg_day7_pasi_val) / avg_day7_pasi_val * 100.0,
        None # Return None if avg_day7_pasi_val is null or zero, indicating undefined PIR
    )
)

# Step 1: Filter treatments that ended in 2022 and extract the insurance type from the associated patient.
# We create an intermediate field 'treatment_ins_type' for partitioning.
treatments_for_partition = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(YEAR(end_dt) == 2022).CALCULATE(

)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered treatments by insurance type.
# For each insurance type, calculate:
#   - The average day 100 PASI score. This is done by averaging 'day100_pasi_score'
#     from the 'outcome_records' of the treatments within each insurance type group.
#   - The number of distinct patients who received these treatments. This is done by
#     counting distinct 'patient_id' from the 'patient' associated with treatments in each group.
insurance_type_summary = treatments_for_partition.PARTITION(name="ins_type_groups", by=(treatment_ins_type)).CALCULATE(
    insurance_type = treatment_ins_type,
    average_pasi_score_day_100 = AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    num_distinct_patients = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient.patient_id)
)

# Step 3: Select the top 5 insurance types.
# The sorting is by the calculated 'average_pasi_score_day_100' in ascending order (lowest first).
final_result = insurance_type_summary.TOP_K(5, by=average_pasi_score_day_100.ASC())

    # Calculate intermediate values for each diagnosis
    diag_name_calc=diag_name,
    max_itch_score_calc=MAX(treatments_for.outcome_records.day100_itch_vas),
    num_distinct_patients_calc=NDISTINCT(treatments_for.patient.patient_id)
).WHERE(
    # Ensure that there is at least one non-null day100_itch_vas score for the diagnosis
    HAS(treatments_for.outcome_records.day100_itch_vas) == 1
).TOP_K(
    3,
    # Sort by the calculated maximum itch score in descending order.
    # Nulls are placed last, though the WHERE clause should prevent nulls here.
    by=max_itch_score_calc.DESC(na_pos='last')
).CALCULATE(
    # Select and rename fields for the final output as requested
    diagnosis_name=diag_name_calc,
    num_patients=num_distinct_patients_calc,
    max_itch_score=max_itch_score_calc
)

[DEBUG] [Q86] Running models for: What are the top 3 doctor specialties by total drug amount prescribed for treatments started in the past 6 calendar months? Return the specialty, number of treatments, and total drug amount.
[DEBUG] [Q86] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q86] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by claude:
# Filter treatments started in the past 6 calendar months
recent_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) <= 6
)

# Group by doctor specialty and calculate metrics
specialty_stats = recent_treatments.PARTITION(name="specialties", by=doctor.speciality).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor.speciality,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
)

# Get top 3 specialties by total drug amount
result = specialty_stats.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())
[DEBUG] [Q86] DataFrame from claude is None
[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start date for the "past 6 calendar months"
# This calculates the first day of the month that was 6 months ago.
# For example, if 'now' is 2024-07-15, this will be 2024-01-01.
six_months_ago_start_date = DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')

# Step 1: Filter treatments that started within the defined time window
# and calculate the doctor's specialty for grouping.
# The 'doctor.speciality' field is accessed from the related Doctors table.
# 'tot_drug_amt' is a field in the Treatments table.
treatments_in_period_with_specialty = Treatments.WHERE(start_dt >= six_months_ago_start_date).CALCULATE(
    doc_specialty_for_grouping = doctor.speciality
    # We will use Treatments.tot_drug_amt directly in the aggregation later
)

# Step 2: Partition the prepared treatments by doctor's specialty.
# For each specialty, calculate the number of treatments and the sum of total drug amounts.
# COUNT(Treatments) and SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt) refer to the records from the original
# Treatments collection that fall into the current partition group and satisfy the initial WHERE clause.
specialty_performance_metrics = treatments_in_period_with_specialty.PARTITION(name="specialty_groups", by=(doc_specialty_for_grouping)).CALCULATE(
    specialty = doc_specialty_for_grouping,  # The name of the doctor's specialty
    number_of_treatments = COUNT(Treatments),  # Total treatments for this specialty in the period
    total_drug_amount = SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)  # Total drug amount prescribed by this specialty in the period
)

# Step 3: Select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount prescribed, in descending order.
top_3_specialties_result = specialty_performance_metrics.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Assign the final result to the 'result' variable
result = top_3_specialties_result

# Define the start date for the 6-month window (e.g., if today is Nov 15, this will be June 1)
six_month_start_date_val = DATETIME('now', '-5 months', 'start of month')

# Filter treatments started within the last 6 calendar months
# and calculate the doctor's specialty and the total drug amount for each treatment.
recent_treatments_info = Treatments.WHERE(start_dt >= six_month_start_date_val).CALCULATE(
    treatment_doc_speciality = doctor.speciality,
    treatment_total_drug_amt = tot_drug_amt
)

# Partition the filtered treatments by the doctor's specialty.
# For each specialty, calculate the number of treatments and the sum of total drug amounts.
specialty_aggregated_data = recent_treatments_info.PARTITION(name="specialty_group", by=(treatment_doc_speciality)).CALCULATE(
    specialty = treatment_doc_speciality,
    number_of_treatments = COUNT(recent_treatments_info),
    total_drug_amount = SUM(recent_treatments_info.treatment_total_drug_amt)
)

# Select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount, in descending order.
result = specialty_aggregated_data.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Define the date boundaries for the last 6 months
date_six_months_ago = DATETIME('now', '-6 months')
current_date = DATETIME('now')

# Filter the Treatments collection based on the specified criteria
filtered_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    (LIKE(LOWER(diagnosis.diag_name), '%psoriasis%')) &  # Condition 1: Diagnosis name contains 'psoriasis' (case-insensitive)
    (drug.fda_appr_dt != None) &                         # Condition 2: The associated drug is FDA-approved (fda_appr_dt is not null)
    (end_dt != None) &                                   # Condition 3a: Treatment has an end date
    (end_dt >= date_six_months_ago) &                    # Condition 3b: Treatment end date is within the last 6 months
    (end_dt <= current_date)                             # Condition 3c: Treatment end date is not in the future
)

# Count the number of treatments that meet all criteria
count_of_psoriasis_treatments = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    num_matching_treatments = COUNT(filtered_treatments)
)

[DEBUG] [Q90] Code generated by gemini:
# Calculate the target registration year (current year - 2)
# This is done at the top level of the DermTreatment graph.
target_year_calculation = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    calculated_target_reg_year=YEAR(DATETIME('now')) - 2
)

# Access Doctors, filter by the calculated registration year, and ensure they have prescribed treatments.
# Then, for each qualifying doctor, find their first treatment and extract the required details.
doctors_registered_two_years_ago = target_year_calculation.Doctors.WHERE(
    (year_reg == calculated_target_reg_year) & (HAS(prescribed_treatments) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    # Select doctor's last name and year of registration
    doc_last_name=last_name,
    doc_year_reg=year_reg,
    # Identify the first treatment record for the doctor
    # RANKING is used per Doctor to find the treatment(s) with the earliest start_dt.
    # allow_ties=True ensures all treatments at the earliest date get rank 1.
    # SINGULAR() is used to pick one record if multiple treatments share the earliest start_dt,
    # or to access the single earliest treatment.
    first_treatment_record=prescribed_treatments.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Doctors", allow_ties=True) == 1
    ).SINGULAR()
).CALCULATE(
    # Re-select the doctor's details for the final output
    doctor_last_name=doc_last_name,
    registration_year=doc_year_reg,
    # Extract the treatment_id and start_dt from the identified first_treatment_record
    first_treatment_id=first_treatment_record.treatment_id,
    first_treatment_date=first_treatment_record.start_dt
)

# Calculate the target registration year (current year - 2)
# This value will be available to subsequent operations on the Doctors collection.
target_year_calculation = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    target_registration_year=YEAR(DATETIME('now')) - 2
)

# Filter doctors by the target registration year and ensure they have treatments.
# Then, for each of these doctors, find their first treatment.
doctors_filtered_and_first_treatment_info = target_year_calculation.Doctors.WHERE(
    (year_reg == target_registration_year) & (HAS(prescribed_treatments) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    # Select doctor's last name and year of registration
    doc_last_name=last_name,
    doc_year_of_registration=year_reg,
    # Identify the first treatment record for the current doctor.
    # RANKING is used to find the treatment(s) with the earliest start_dt.
    # per="Doctors" ensures ranking is done independently for each doctor.
    # allow_ties=True handles cases where multiple treatments might share the same earliest start date.
    # .SINGULAR() is used to pick one record if multiple treatments have the same rank 1 (earliest date).
    first_treatment_record=prescribed_treatments.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Doctors", allow_ties=True) == 1
    ).SINGULAR()
)

# Extract the specific details from the first treatment record.
# This second CALCULATE is needed to access properties of 'first_treatment_record' defined in the previous CALCULATE.
result = doctors_filtered_and_first_treatment_info.CALCULATE(
    doctor_last_name=doc_last_name,
    doctor_year_of_registration=doc_year_of_registration,
    first_treatment_id=first_treatment_record.treatment_id,
    first_treatment_date=first_treatment_record.start_dt
)

[DEBUG] [Q94] Running models for: Calculate the CPUR for each merchant, considering only successful transactions. Return the merchant name and CPUR.CPUR (coupon usage rate) = number of distinct coupons used / number of distinct transactions

[DEBUG] [Q94] Code generated by claude:
# Get all successful transactions for each merchant (both sent and received)
merchant_transaction_info = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    successful_transactions_sent=transactions_sent.WHERE(status == "successful"),
    successful_transactions_received=transactions_received.WHERE(status == "successful")
)

# Calculate CPUR for each merchant
result = merchant_transaction_info.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    total_successful_transactions=COUNT(successful_transactions_sent) + COUNT(successful_transactions_received),
    distinct_coupons_used=NDISTINCT(successful_transactions_sent.coupon_id) + NDISTINCT(successful_transactions_received.coupon_id),
    cpur=IFF(
        (COUNT(successful_transactions_sent) + COUNT(successful_transactions_received)) > 0,
        (NDISTINCT(successful_transactions_sent.coupon_id) + NDISTINCT(successful_transactions_received.coupon_id)) / 
        (COUNT(successful_transactions_sent) + COUNT(successful_transactions_received)),
        0
    )
).WHERE(total_successful_transactions > 0)

    num_successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_received.WHERE(status == "completed"))
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    cpur=IFF(
        num_successful_transactions == 0,
        0.0,
        num_distinct_coupons_used / num_successful_transactions
    )

# Get all successful transactions for each merchant (both sent and received)
merchant_transaction_info = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    # Combine sent and received transactions, filter for successful ones
    successful_sent_transactions=transactions_sent.WHERE(status == "SUCCESS"),
    successful_received_transactions=transactions_received.WHERE(status == "SUCCESS")
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    # Count distinct successful transactions (both sent and received)
    total_successful_transactions=NDISTINCT(successful_sent_transactions.txid) + NDISTINCT(successful_received_transactions.txid),
    # Count distinct coupons used in successful transactions
    distinct_coupons_used=NDISTINCT(successful_sent_transactions.WHERE(coupon_id != None).coupon_id) + NDISTINCT(successful_received_transactions.WHERE(coupon_id != None).coupon_id)
).WHERE(total_successful_transactions > 0)

result = merchant_transaction_info.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    cpur=distinct_coupons_used * 1.0 / total_successful_transactions
)

[DEBUG] [Q94] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Calculate intermediate counts for successful transactions per merchant.
# We access Merchants through the Ewallet graph.
# For each merchant, we filter their received transactions for those with status 'completed'.
# Then, we count the number of distinct coupon IDs used in these successful transactions
# and the total number of distinct successful transactions.
merchant_stats = Ewallet.Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name_val = name,
    num_distinct_coupons_val = NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(status == 'completed').coupon_id),
    num_distinct_txs_val = COUNT(transactions_received.WHERE(status == 'completed').txid)
)

# Step 2: Calculate CPUR using the counts from Step 1 and select the final fields.
# We use the IFF function to handle cases where a merchant might have zero successful transactions,
# setting CPUR to 0.0 in such cases to prevent division by zero.
# We ensure floating-point division by multiplying the numerator by 1.0.
result = merchant_stats.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name = merchant_name_val,
    CPUR = IFF(
        num_distinct_txs_val > 0,
        (num_distinct_coupons_val * 1.0) / num_distinct_txs_val,
        0.0
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q95] Running models for: For users in the US and Canada, how many total notifications were sent in each of the last 3 weeks excluding the current week? How many of those were sent on weekends?Weekends are Saturdays and Sundays. Truncate created_at to week for aggregation.

[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by claude:
# Filter notifications for US and Canada users from last 3 weeks (excluding current week)
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ("US", "Canada"))
).notifications.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1 &
    DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3
).CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))  # Saturday=7, Sunday=1
)

# Group by week and calculate metrics
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week_start=week_start,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q95] DataFrame from claude is None
[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
# Define time boundaries at the Ewallet graph level
graph_context = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    current_week_start_val = DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    # three_weeks_ago_start_val is the start of the week that began 3 weeks prior to the current week's start.
    # For example, if the current week starts on Monday, Oct 23rd:
    # - DATETIME('now', 'start of week') is Mon, Oct 23rd.
    # - DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of week'), '-3 weeks') is Mon, Oct 2nd.
    # The period of interest is [Mon, Oct 2nd; Mon, Oct 9th; Mon, Oct 16th].
    three_weeks_ago_start_val = DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of week'), '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications for users in US/Canada, calculate week information, and filter by the defined time window.
# The created_at timestamps are truncated to the start of their respective weeks for aggregation.
user_notifications_with_week_info = graph_context.Notifications.WHERE(
    ISIN(user.country, ("US", "Canada")) # Filter for users in US or Canada
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to the start of the week for grouping
    notification_processing_week = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Flag (1 for weekend, 0 for weekday) for easy summation
    is_weekend_notification_flag = IFF(ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday")), 1, 0)
).WHERE(
    # Filter for notifications within the last 3 full weeks (excluding the current week)
    (notification_processing_week >= graph_context.three_weeks_ago_start_val) &
    (notification_processing_week < graph_context.current_week_start_val)
)

# Group by the calculated week and aggregate counts
weekly_notification_summary = user_notifications_with_week_info.PARTITION(
    name="notifications_grouped_by_week", by=(notification_processing_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date = notification_processing_week, # The unique week identifier (start date of the week)
    total_notifications_sent = COUNT(Notifications), # Total notifications in this week for the filtered users
    weekend_notifications_sent = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend_notification_flag) # Total weekend notifications in this week
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.DESC()) # Order by week, most recent first

result = weekly_notification_summary

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the week 3 weeks ago
date_refs = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    current_week_start_ts = DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    three_weeks_ago_start_ts = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter users from US or Canada
users_in_target_countries = date_refs.Users.WHERE(ISIN(country, ("US", "Canada")))

# Get notifications for these users and filter them by the defined date range
relevant_notifications = users_in_target_countries.notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= three_weeks_ago_start_ts) & (created_at < current_week_start_ts)
)

notifications_with_details = relevant_notifications.CALCULATE(
    notification_week_val = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend_notification = ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition by the notification week
weekly_notification_groups = notifications_with_details.PARTITION(
    name="notifications_per_week", by=(notification_week_val)
)

# Calculate total and weekend notifications for each week
weekly_summary = weekly_notification_groups.CALCULATE(
    week_start = notification_week_val,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(IFF(is_weekend_notification, 1, 0))
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())

result = weekly_summary

# Get transactions from US users in the last 7 days
us_user_transactions = Users.WHERE(country == "US").CALCULATE(
    sent_transactions_last_7_days=transactions_sent.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
    ),
    received_transactions_last_7_days=transactions_received.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
    )
)

    num_transactions=COUNT(us_user_transactions.sent_transactions_last_7_days) + 
                    COUNT(us_user_transactions.received_transactions_last_7_days),
    total_amount=SUM(us_user_transactions.sent_transactions_last_7_days.amount) +

# Define the start of the 7-day period (inclusive of today).
# This means from 6 days ago (at 00:00:00 local time for 'now') up to the current time 'now'.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', '-6 days', 'start of day')

# and then filter those transactions that occurred within the last 7 days.

# Calculate the total number of these transactions and their total amount.

    num_transactions = COUNT(filtered_transactions),

[DEBUG] [Q98] Code generated by claude:
# Filter user balances updated in the past week (inclusive of 7 days ago)
recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
)

# Calculate average balance per user for the past week
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(name="users", by=user_id).CALCULATE(
    user_avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Calculate the overall average AMB
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_amb=AVG(user_avg_balances.user_avg_balance)
)
DATEDIFF unsupported for 'DAYS'.
[DEBUG] [Q98] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q98] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter UserBalances for records updated in the past 7 days (inclusive)
# DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, 'now') gives the number of full days between updated_at and now.
# We want records from 7 days ago up to now, so the difference should be less than or equal to 7.
balances_in_last_week = Ewallet.UserBalances.WHERE(DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7)

# Step 2: For each user, calculate their average balance (AMB) from these filtered records.
# We partition by user_id to group balance records by user.
# Then, for each user group, we calculate the average of their balances.
amb_per_user_calculation = balances_in_last_week.PARTITION(name="user_balances_group", by=(user_id)).CALCULATE(
    user_id=user_id,  # Include user_id from partition key
    user_amb_value=AVG(UserBalances.balance)  # Calculate average balance for this user's records in the group
)

# Step 3: Calculate the average of these per-user AMBs.
# This gives the final "average balance" requested, which is the average of individual user AMBs.
average_amb_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_balance=AVG(amb_per_user_calculation.user_amb_value)
)

# Step 1: Calculate the total revenue for each merchant from successful transactions.
# We filter transactions_received for status 'success' and receiver_type 1.
merchant_revenues = Ewallet.Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merch_name=name,  # Temporarily store merchant's name
    merch_category=category,  # Temporarily store merchant's category
    calculated_revenue=SUM(
        transactions_received.WHERE(
            (status == "success") & (receiver_type == 1)
        ).amount
    )
)

# Step 2: Rank merchants based on their calculated revenue and select the final output fields.
# The rank is determined by 'calculated_revenue' in descending order.
result = merchant_revenues.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merch_name,
    category=merch_category,
    revenue_amount=calculated_revenue,
    revenue_rank=RANKING(by=calculated_revenue.DESC())
)

# Calculate total revenue for each merchant from successfully completed transactions
# where the receiver_type indicates a merchant.
merchant_revenues = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    m_name=name,
    m_category=category,
    total_revenue=DEFAULT_TO(
        SUM(
            transactions_received.WHERE(
                (status == "completed") & (receiver_type == 1)
            ).amount
        ),
        0.0  # Default to 0.0 revenue if no qualifying transactions
    )
)

# Filter transactions for user senders in the last 2 months (excluding current month)
user_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 2)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_id=sender_id
)

# Group by month and calculate PMDAU (distinct senders per month)
result = user_transactions.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q102] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Define date boundaries using Ewallet (the top-level graph collection)
date_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Timestamp for the start of the current month (e.g., if today is July 15th, this is July 1st, 00:00:00)
    current_month_start_ts=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    # Timestamp for the start of the month two months prior to the current month
    # (e.g., if current_month_start_ts is July 1st, this is May 1st, 00:00:00)
    two_months_ago_start_ts=DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of month'), '-2 months')
)

# Filter transactions:
# - sender_type must be 0 (user)
# - created_at must be within the last two full months (excluding the current month)
# Transactions from two_months_ago_start_ts (inclusive) up to current_month_start_ts (exclusive)
active_user_transactions_in_period = date_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (created_at >= two_months_ago_start_ts) &
    (created_at < current_month_start_ts)
)

# Prepare data for daily aggregation:
# Extract a timestamp for the start of the transaction's month,
# a timestamp for the start of the transaction's day, and the sender_id.
daily_input_data = active_user_transactions_in_period.CALCULATE(
    transaction_year_month_ts=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'), # e.g., 2023-05-01 00:00:00
    transaction_day_ts=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day'),         # e.g., 2023-05-15 00:00:00
    user_sender_id=sender_id                                         # The ID of the sending user
)

# Calculate Daily Active Users (DAU):
# Partition by the transaction's month and day, then count distinct senders for each day.
dau_per_day_counts = daily_input_data.PARTITION(
    name="day_activity_group",  # Name for the group of transactions within a specific day
    by=(transaction_year_month_ts, transaction_day_ts) # Grouping keys: month and day
).CALCULATE(
    # Retain the grouping keys for the next step
    calc_year_month_ts=transaction_year_month_ts,
    # calc_day_ts=transaction_day_ts, # Not strictly needed for the final PMDAU calculation
    # Count distinct users (senders) for this specific day
    daily_active_users_count=NDISTINCT(daily_input_data.user_sender_id)
)

# Calculate PMDAU (Per Month Daily Active Users):
# Partition the DAU counts by month, then average the daily DAU counts for each month.
pmdau_by_month = dau_per_day_counts.PARTITION(
    name="month_dau_group", # Name for the group of daily DAU counts within a specific month
    by=calc_year_month_ts   # Grouping key: month
).CALCULATE(
    # The month for which PMDAU is calculated
    report_month=calc_year_month_ts,
    # Average the daily_active_users_count for all days within this month that had activity
    pmdau=AVG(month_dau_group.daily_active_users_count)
)

# Assign the final result
result = pmdau_by_month

[DEBUG] [Q102] DataFrame from claude is valid
[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start of the current month and the start of the period two months ago.
# For example, if today is July 15th:
# - current_month_start_dt will be July 1st, 00:00:00.
# - two_months_ago_start_dt will be May 1st, 00:00:00.
# The filter will include transactions from May 1st up to (but not including) July 1st.
current_month_start_dt = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
two_months_ago_start_dt = DATETIME(current_month_start_dt, '-2 months')

# 1. Filter transactions for sender_type = 0 and within the last two full months.
#    Also, prepare data for daily aggregation by extracting day and month from created_at.
transactions_for_dau_calculation = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (created_at >= two_months_ago_start_dt) &
    (created_at < current_month_start_dt)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_day_ts = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day'),     # Timestamp for the specific day of transaction
    transaction_month_ts = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'), # Timestamp for the month of transaction
    user_sender_id = sender_id                                     # Sender ID for distinct counting
)

# 2. Calculate Daily Active Users (DAU).
#    Group by the specific day (transaction_day_ts) and the month it belongs to (transaction_month_ts).
#    For each day, count the number of distinct senders.
dau_by_day_calculation = transactions_for_dau_calculation.PARTITION(
    name="daily_active_users_group", by=(transaction_day_ts, transaction_month_ts)
).CALCULATE(
    day_key = transaction_day_ts,          # The day for which DAU is calculated
    month_key = transaction_month_ts,      # The month this day belongs to (carried for next step)
    daily_active_count = NDISTINCT(transactions_for_dau_calculation.user_sender_id) # Distinct active users for this day
)

# 3. Calculate Per Month Daily Active Users (PMDAU).
#    Group the daily DAU counts by month (month_key).
#    For each month, calculate the average of the daily_active_count.
pmdau_final_calculation = dau_by_day_calculation.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_pmdau_group", by=(month_key)
).CALCULATE(
    result_month = month_key,  # The month for which PMDAU is reported
    pmdau = AVG(dau_by_day_calculation.daily_active_count) # Average of DAUs for that month
).ORDER_BY(result_month.ASC()) # Order results by month ascending

result = pmdau_final_calculation

[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start and end timestamps for the previous month
previous_month_start_dt = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 months')
current_month_start_dt = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions that occurred in the previous month
transactions_in_prev_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= previous_month_start_dt) & (created_at < current_month_start_dt)
)

# Calculate the number of successful transactions and total transactions in the previous month
# Then, calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR)
str_calculation_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    num_successful_transactions = COUNT(transactions_in_prev_month.WHERE(status == 'completed')),
    num_total_transactions = COUNT(transactions_in_prev_month)
).CALCULATE(
    # STR = (successful transactions / total transactions) * 100
    # Handle division by zero by returning 0.0 if there are no total transactions
    previous_month_str = IFF(num_total_transactions > 0, (num_successful_transactions * 100.0) / num_total_transactions, 0.0)
)

# Assign the final STR value to the result variable
result = str_calculation_result.CALCULATE(previous_month_str)

[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start and end boundaries for the previous month dynamically.
# prev_month_start_boundary is the first moment of the previous month.
# curr_month_start_boundary is the first moment of the current month.
prev_month_start_boundary = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 months')
curr_month_start_boundary = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions that occurred strictly within the previous month.
transactions_in_prev_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= prev_month_start_boundary) & (created_at < curr_month_start_boundary)
)

# Count the number of successful transactions in the previous month.
# We assume a transaction is 'successful' if its status is 'completed'.
num_successful_tx_prev_month = COUNT(
    transactions_in_prev_month.WHERE(status == 'completed')
)

# Count the total number of transactions in the previous month.
total_tx_prev_month = COUNT(transactions_in_prev_month)

# Calculate STR (Success Transaction Rate) for the previous month.
# STR = (Number of successful transactions / Total number of transactions) * 100.
# If total_tx_prev_month is 0, STR is 0.0 to avoid division by zero.
calculated_str = IFF(
    total_tx_prev_month > 0,
    (num_successful_tx_prev_month * 100.0) / total_tx_prev_month,
    0.0
)

# Assign the calculated STR to a field in the Ewallet collection.
# The result will be a single row with the STR for the previous month.
wallet_str_previous_month = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    success_transaction_rate_previous_month = calculated_str
)

# Filter transactions for 2023, successful status, and sent by active users
user_transactions_2023 = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "successful") &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 represents users, not merchants
).CALCULATE(
    month_date = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_user_id = sender_id
)

# Group by month and count distinct active users who sent money

    month_date = month_date,

WITH _s2 AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT
    DATE(created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND sender_type = 1
    AND status = 'successful'
), _s3 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT users.uid) AS agg_0,
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily AS wallet_transactions_daily
  JOIN main.users AS users
    ON users.status = 'active' AND users.uid = wallet_transactions_daily.sender_id
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', wallet_transactions_daily.created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.sender_type = 1
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.status = 'successful'
  GROUP BY
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month')
)
SELECT
  _s2.month_date,
  COALESCE(_s3.agg_0, 0) AS distinct_active_users
FROM _s2 AS _s2
LEFT JOIN _s3 AS _s3
  ON _s2.month_date = _s3.month_date
ORDER BY
  month_date

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only those made in 2023, completed,
# and sent by an active user. Also, calculate the start of the transaction month.
transactions_for_analysis = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "completed") &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &  # Ensures the sender is a user
    (sending_user.status == "active")  # Ensures the sending user is active
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the first day of the month for grouping
    transaction_month_as_date = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
    # Note: sending_user.uid will be accessed in the next step for NDISTINCT
)

# Step 2: Partition by the calculated month_date and count distinct active sending users.
# The items being partitioned are from 'transactions_for_analysis'.
# Each item in a partition is a transaction that met the criteria for that month.
result = transactions_for_analysis.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_active_user_sends", by=(transaction_month_as_date)
).CALCULATE(
    # 'transaction_month_as_date' is the partition key, representing the month as a date
    month = transaction_month_as_date,
    # Count distinct user UIDs from the 'Transactions' collection within the current partition.
    # These transactions have already been filtered to ensure the sender was an active user.
    num_distinct_active_users = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.uid)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

WITH _s2 AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT
    DATE(created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND sender_type = 1
    AND status = 'successful'
), _s3 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT users.uid) AS agg_0,
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily AS wallet_transactions_daily
  JOIN main.users AS users
    ON users.status = 'active' AND users.uid = wallet_transactions_daily.sender_id
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', wallet_transactions_daily.created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.sender_type = 1
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.status = 'successful'
  GROUP BY
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month')
)
SELECT
  _s2.month_date,
  COALESCE(_s3.agg_0, 0) AS distinct_active_users
FROM _s2 AS _s2
LEFT JOIN _s3 AS _s3
  ON _s2.month_date = _s3.month_date
ORDER BY
  month_date

[DEBUG] [Q110] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only those from 2023, completed, sent by an active user.
filtered_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &  # Transaction occurred in 2023
    (status == 'completed') &  # Transaction was successful
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &  # Sender is a user (not a merchant)
    (sending_user.status == 'active')  # Sending user is active
)

# Step 2: For the filtered transactions, calculate the transaction month (as the first day of the month)
# and get the UID of the sending user.
transactions_info = filtered_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month_date = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),  # Get the first day of the transaction month
    user_sender_uid = sending_user.uid  # Get the UID of the active sending user
)

# Step 3: Group by the transaction month date and count the number of distinct active users (senders).
monthly_distinct_active_senders = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_user_transactions", by=(transaction_month_date)
).CALCULATE(
    month = transaction_month_date,  # The month, represented as a date (first day of the month)
    num_distinct_active_users = NDISTINCT(user_sender_uid)  # Count of distinct active users who sent money
)

# Step 4: Order the results by month in ascending order (earliest month first).
result = monthly_distinct_active_senders.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Filter transactions for 2023, successful status, and sent by active users
user_transactions_2023 = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "successful") &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 represents users, not merchants
).CALCULATE(
    month_date = DATETIME(created_at, "start of month"),
    sender_user_id = sender_id
)

# Group by month and count distinct active users who sent money
result = user_transactions_2023.PARTITION(name="months", by=month_date).CALCULATE(
    month_date = month_date,
    distinct_active_users = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.WHERE(status == "active").uid)
).ORDER_BY(month_date.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q110] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1 & 2: Filter active users and access their sent transactions.
# Step 3: Filter these transactions for the year 2023 and 'completed' status.
relevant_transactions = Users.WHERE(status == 'active').transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & (status == 'completed')
)

# Step 4: Calculate the start of the month for each transaction and carry over sender_id.
# sender_id from transactions_sent by a User is effectively the User's uid.
transactions_for_grouping = relevant_transactions.CALCULATE(
    month_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    user_id_of_sender = sender_id
)

# Step 5: Partition by the calculated month_start.
# Step 6: For each month, count the number of distinct active users who sent transactions.
monthly_active_senders = transactions_for_grouping.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_tx_group", by=(month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month = month_start,
    num_active_users = NDISTINCT(monthly_tx_group.user_id_of_sender)
)

# Step 7: Order the results by month in ascending order.
result = monthly_active_senders.ORDER_BY(transaction_month.ASC())

# and calculate necessary details: sender's country, sender's UID, and transaction amount.
# The 'sending_user' relationship links a transaction to the Users collection.
transaction_details_for_senders = Transactions.WHERE(sender_type == 0).CALCULATE(
    sender_country_field = sending_user.country,
    sender_user_id_field = sending_user.uid,
    transaction_amount_field = amount
)

# Step 2: Partition the transaction details by the sender's country.
# For each country, aggregate the number of distinct sending users and the sum of transaction amounts.
country_level_summary = transaction_details_for_senders.PARTITION(name="country_group", by=(sender_country_field)).CALCULATE(

    distinct_sending_users_count = NDISTINCT(transaction_details_for_senders.sender_user_id_field),

)

    name="country_partition", by=(country)  # 'country' refers to Users.country
).CALCULATE(
    # Store the country name from the partition key for output.
    country_name_intermediate = country,
    # Count the number of distinct users in this country partition.
    # As users_with_qualifying_transactions contains unique users who made relevant transactions,
    # COUNT(Users) here gives the number of distinct users for this country.

    # Sum the 'amount' of transactions_sent where sender_type is 0
    # for all users within this country partition.
    total_transaction_amount_intermediate = SUM(Users.transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)
)

    number_of_distinct_users_who_sent = num_distinct_users_intermediate,

[DEBUG] [Q120] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter merchants to get active ones in the 'retail' category.
active_retail_merchants = Merchants.WHERE(
    (status == "active") & CONTAINS(category, "retail")
)

# Step 2: For these merchants, access their balance records.
# Step 3: Filter balance records to include only those from "today".
# Step 4: From today's balance records, select the latest one for each merchant.
# The RANKING function is used per merchant to find their single most recent balance entry for today.
latest_today_merchant_balances = active_retail_merchants.balances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) == 0
).WHERE(
    RANKING(by=updated_at.DESC(), per="Merchants", allow_ties=False) == 1
)

# Step 5: Calculate the median of the 'balance' from these selected records.
# The result will be a single value representing the median balance.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    median_retail_merchant_balance_today = MEDIAN(latest_today_merchant_balances.balance)
)

# Step 1: Filter merchants to find those that are active and have 'retail' in their category.
# We use LOWER() on the category field to ensure the 'contains' check is case-insensitive.
active_retail_merchants = Merchants.WHERE(
    (status == 'active') & CONTAINS(LOWER(category), 'retail')
)

# Step 2: Access the balances of these filtered merchants and further filter for balances updated today.
# DATE(updated_at) extracts the date part of the timestamp.
# DATE(DATETIME('now')) provides the current date.
todays_balances_for_active_retail_merchants = active_retail_merchants.balances.WHERE(
    DATE(updated_at) == DATE(DATETIME('now'))
)

# Step 3: Calculate the median of the 'balance' from the filtered balances.
# This will result in a single value: the median balance for the specified group of merchants today.
median_balance_output = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    median_merchant_balance_today = MEDIAN(todays_balances_for_active_retail_merchants.balance)

)

WITH _s2 AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT
    DATE(created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND sender_type = 1
    AND status = 'successful'
), _s3 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT(DISTINCT users.uid) AS agg_0,
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month') AS month_date
  FROM main.wallet_transactions_daily AS wallet_transactions_daily
  JOIN main.users AS users
    ON users.status = 'active' AND users.uid = wallet_transactions_daily.sender_id
  WHERE
    CAST(STRFTIME('%Y', wallet_transactions_daily.created_at) AS INTEGER) = 2023
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.sender_type = 1
    AND wallet_transactions_daily.status = 'successful'
  GROUP BY
    DATE(wallet_transactions_daily.created_at, 'start of month')
)
SELECT
  _s2.month_date,
  COALESCE(_s3.agg_0, 0) AS distinct_active_users
FROM _s2 AS _s2
LEFT JOIN _s3 AS _s3
  ON _s2.month_date = _s3.month_date
ORDER BY
  month_date
